(optional helper)., struct EditDocument: Codable, Sendable, Equatable — { version: Int; rawDevelop: RAWDevelopSettings; adjustments: [AdjustmentNode]; lut: LUTSettings }., struct RAWDevelopSettings: Codable, Sendable, Equatable — optional CIRAWFilter knobs; static let neutral; func apply(to: CIRAWFilter); var isNeutral: Bool., enum AdjustmentNode: Codable, Sendable, Equatable — closed set of CIFilter-backed cases; func apply(to: CIImage) -> CIImage., struct LUTSettings: Codable, Sendable, Equatable — { lutID: LUTID?; intensity: Double }; static let none., struct LUTID: Codable, Sendable, Equatable, Hashable — { raw: UUID }., struct ImageSource: Sendable, Equatable — { url: URL; kind: Kind(.raw/.standard); nativeExtent: CGSize }., enum RenderScale: Sendable, Equatable — .preview(maxSize: CGSize) / .full., enum WorkingSpace: String, Codable, Sendable, Equatable — .sRGB/.displayP3; var cgColorSpace; static let current., enum RenderPipeline — namespace; static func buildImage(source:document:lut:scale:space:) -> CIImage? (pure, called only inside the actor)., ExportFormat (promoted from ImageProcessor.ExportFormat to top-level enum: String, CaseIterable, Identifiable, Sendable)., ImageError (made Sendable; already an enum with String/no associated non-Sendable values).

**Implementation:**

## Concurrency / Sendable Hardening — Implementation Spec

Scope: ONLY the concurrency/Sendable seam. The data-model fields, pipeline stages, intensity blend math, and color-space details are owned by sibling components; this spec defines them just enough to make the boundary crossings provably safe and references them where another component is authoritative.

### 0. The exact Swift-6 violations in today's code (verified against source)

1. **Non-Sendable singleton crossing an actor boundary.** `final class ImageProcessor` (ImageProcessor.swift:9) is not `Sendable` and holds `private let context: CIContext` (line 13, non-Sendable). `AppViewModel` stores `private let processor = ImageProcessor.shared` (AppViewModel.swift:48) on a `@MainActor` class, then captures it into `Task.detached { [processor, exportFormat] in ... }` (AppViewModel.swift:297). Capturing a non-Sendable class instance into a `Task.detached` closure is a hard error under `SWIFT_STRICT_CONCURRENCY=complete`.

2. **Non-Sendable `CIImage` crossing the boundary.** Same closure captures `imageToExport` (a `CIImage`, AppViewModel.swift:283/299). `CIImage` is not `Sendable`.

3. **Implicit `self` capture across the boundary.** Inside the detached closures, `await MainActor.run { self.isExporting = ... }` (AppViewModel.swift:300-308) and the derive equivalents (AppViewModel.swift:378-397) capture `self` (a `@MainActor` class) into a non-isolated closure. The `deriveRecipe` progress callback `progress: { progress, stage in Task { @MainActor in self.deriveProgress = ... } }` (AppViewModel.swift:348-353) also captures `self` and is itself passed into a detached job.

4. **Heavy work on the main actor.** `previewTask = Task { ... lut.apply(to: source) ... }` (AppViewModel.swift:236) is a *non-detached* `Task` inside a `@MainActor` method, so it inherits `@MainActor`; `CubeLUT.apply` (the filter-graph build) and the downstream `renderPreview` `createCGImage` run on the main thread. Not a compile error, but the responsiveness bug the actor migration fixes for free.

5. **Two more boundary sites.** `ImageCollection` runs thumbnail work in `Task.detached` touching `ImageProcessor.shared` (per subsystem map, ImageCollection.swift ~:40); `FilmstripView` calls `processor.generateThumbnail` (FilmstripView.swift ~:88). Both must move behind the actor.

The fix strategy is uniform: **the GPU (CIContext + every CIImage/CIFilter) lives only inside `actor RenderEngine`; only `Sendable` values enter, only `Sendable` `CGImage?`/`Data`/`Void` leave.**

---

### 1. `RenderEngine` — the isolation domain (NEW file)

```swift
import CoreImage
import CoreGraphics
import Metal
import ImageIO

/// The ONE actor that owns GPU state. CIContext / CIImage / CIFilter are created
/// and consumed entirely inside this actor and never escape, which is what makes
/// the whole render path Swift-6 strict-concurrency clean. Replaces the GPU duties
/// of the former non-Sendable `ImageProcessor` singleton.
actor RenderEngine {
    static let shared = RenderEngine()

    private let context: CIContext
    private var lutRegistry: [LUTID: CubeLUT] = [:]

    init() {
        if let device = MTLCreateSystemDefaultDevice() {
            context = CIContext(mtlDevice: device)
        } else {
            context = CIContext()
        }
        // NOTE: kCIContextWorkingColorSpace deliberately left at CI default — see colorSeam open question.
    }

    // MARK: LUT registry (resolves the Sendable LUTID in EditDocument → the heavier CubeLUT)
    func register(_ lut: CubeLUT, for id: LUTID) { lutRegistry[id] = lut }
    func unregister(_ id: LUTID) { lutRegistry[id] = nil }

    // MARK: Preview — ONE definition, scaled. Returns Sendable CGImage.
    func renderPreview(source: ImageSource,
                       document: EditDocument,
                       maxSize: CGSize,
                       space: WorkingSpace) -> CGImage? {
        let lut = document.lut.lutID.flatMap { lutRegistry[$0] }
        guard let img = RenderPipeline.buildImage(
            source: source, document: document, lut: lut,
            scale: .preview(maxSize: maxSize), space: space) else { return nil }
        // EXPLICIT colorSpace — fixes the implicit-default raster at old ImageProcessor.swift:109.
        return context.createCGImage(img, from: img.extent,
                                     format: .RGBA8, colorSpace: space.cgColorSpace)
    }

    // MARK: Export — SAME definition, full-res. Writes inside the actor; only Void/throws crosses out.
    func export(source: ImageSource,
                document: EditDocument,
                to url: URL,
                format: ExportFormat,
                space: WorkingSpace,
                quality: CGFloat = 0.95) throws {
        let lut = document.lut.lutID.flatMap { lutRegistry[$0] }
        guard let img = RenderPipeline.buildImage(
            source: source, document: document, lut: lut,
            scale: .full, space: space) else { throw ImageError.processingFailed }
        let cs = space.cgColorSpace
        let data: Data?
        switch format {
        case .tiff: data = context.tiffRepresentation(of: img, format: .RGBA16, colorSpace: cs)
        case .png:  data = context.pngRepresentation(of: img,  format: .RGBA8,  colorSpace: cs)
        case .jpeg: data = context.jpegRepresentation(of: img, colorSpace: cs,
                        options: [CIImageRepresentationOption(rawValue: kCGImageDestinationLossyCompressionQuality as String): quality])
        }
        guard let d = data else { throw ImageError.exportFailed }
        try d.write(to: url)
    }

    // MARK: Thumbnails — moved off ImageProcessor; CGImageSource path lives behind the actor.
    func thumbnail(url: URL, maxPixelSize: Int = 240) -> CGImage? {
        guard let src = CGImageSourceCreateWithURL(url as CFURL, nil) else { return nil }
        return Self.thumbnail(from: src, maxPixelSize: maxPixelSize)
    }
    func thumbnail(data: Data, maxPixelSize: Int = 240) -> CGImage? {
        guard let src = CGImageSourceCreateWithData(data as CFData, nil) else { return nil }
        return Self.thumbnail(from: src, maxPixelSize: maxPixelSize)
    }
    private static func thumbnail(from src: CGImageSource, maxPixelSize: Int) -> CGImage? {
        let opts: [CFString: Any] = [
            kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailFromImageAlways: true,
            kCGImageSourceThumbnailMaxPixelSize: maxPixelSize,
            kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailWithTransform: true,
        ]
        return CGImageSourceCreateThumbnailAtIndex(src, 0, opts as CFDictionary)
    }
}
```

Why `CGImage` out, not bytes / not `NSImage`: `CGImage` is an immutable CoreFoundation type that is safe to hand across the boundary (treated as `Sendable` in practice; if the compiler flags it under complete checking, wrap the return in a tiny `struct RenderedImage: @unchecked Sendable { let cg: CGImage }` — but prefer plain `CGImage?`, which Apple's own concurrency-annotated headers treat as sendable for these APIs). `NSImage` is *not* `Sendable` and is `@MainActor`-affine for drawing, so it must be constructed on the main actor: `NSImage(cgImage: cg, size: ...)`. Returning bytes (the Approach-3 `RenderResult`) is rejected: it forces a copy + an NSImage rebuild every preview tick.

Concurrency note on serialization: an `actor` serializes calls, so two rapid preview requests queue rather than racing the `CIContext`. That is exactly the safety we want; combined with the VM-side debounce (Section 4) it does not become a latency problem.

---

### 2. Sendable value state (NEW file `EditDocument.swift`)

These are the ONLY things that cross into the actor (plus `URL`, `CGSize`, `CGFloat`, all already `Sendable`). Full field lists belong to the data-model component; here is the Sendable-relevant shape and the conformances that make the boundary safe.

```swift
struct EditDocument: Codable, Sendable, Equatable {
    var version: Int = 1
    var rawDevelop: RAWDevelopSettings = .neutral
    var adjustments: [AdjustmentNode] = []
    var lut: LUTSettings = .none
}

struct RAWDevelopSettings: Codable, Sendable, Equatable {
    var exposure: Double? = nil
    var neutralTemperature: Double? = nil
    var neutralTint: Double? = nil
    // … remaining optional knobs (data-model component owns the full list)
    static let neutral = RAWDevelopSettings()
    var isNeutral: Bool { self == .neutral }
    func apply(to f: CIRAWFilter) { /* set only non-nil keys; see RAW-develop component */ }
}

enum AdjustmentNode: Codable, Sendable, Equatable {
    case exposure(ev: Double)
    case colorControls(brightness: Double, contrast: Double, saturation: Double)
    case highlightShadow(highlights: Double, shadows: Double)
    case temperatureTint(temp: Double, tint: Double)
    case vibrance(amount: Double)
    func apply(to image: CIImage) -> CIImage { /* CIFilter wrappers; pipeline component owns bodies */ }
}

struct LUTSettings: Codable, Sendable, Equatable {
    var lutID: LUTID? = nil
    var intensity: Double = 1.0
    static let none = LUTSettings()
}

struct LUTID: Codable, Sendable, Equatable, Hashable { let raw: UUID }
```

Sendable rationale: every stored property is a value type composed of `Double`/`Int`/`Bool`/`UUID`/optionals/arrays of `Sendable` — so `Sendable` is *synthesized*, no `@unchecked` needed. The closed `enum AdjustmentNode` (vs `[any AdjustmentNode]`) is load-bearing for THIS component: an existential array cannot synthesize `Sendable`/`Codable`/`Equatable` and would force a manual `@unchecked Sendable` box, defeating the purpose.

`RAWDevelopSettings.apply(to:)` and `AdjustmentNode.apply(to:)` take/return non-Sendable `CIImage`/`CIRAWFilter`, so they MUST be invoked only from inside the actor (they are, via `RenderPipeline.buildImage`). They are pure functions with no shared state, so they need no isolation themselves; just never call them from `@MainActor`.

`RenderTypes.swift` (may be folded into the above file):

```swift
struct ImageSource: Sendable, Equatable {
    enum Kind: Sendable, Equatable { case raw, standard }
    let url: URL
    let kind: Kind
    let nativeExtent: CGSize
}
enum RenderScale: Sendable, Equatable { case preview(maxSize: CGSize); case full }
enum WorkingSpace: String, Codable, Sendable, Equatable {
    case sRGB, displayP3
    var cgColorSpace: CGColorSpace {
        switch self {
        case .sRGB:      return CGColorSpace(name: CGColorSpace.sRGB)!
        case .displayP3: return CGColorSpace(name: CGColorSpace.displayP3)!
        }
    }
    static let current: WorkingSpace = .sRGB
}
```

---

### 3. `RenderPipeline` (NEW file) — pure, actor-internal

`enum RenderPipeline { static func buildImage(source:document:lut:scale:space:) -> CIImage? }`. It constructs `CIRAWFilter`/`CIImage`/applies nodes/LUT and returns an un-rendered `CIImage`. It is a free function with no captured state. CONCURRENCY CONSTRAINT: it returns a non-Sendable `CIImage`, so it must be called **only** from inside `RenderEngine` (it is, in renderPreview/export). Do not expose it to `AppViewModel`. The body (stages, scaleFactor, intensity) is owned by the render-pipeline component; this spec only fixes where it is allowed to run.

---

### 4. `AppViewModel` rewrite (MODIFIED) — the boundary callers

State change:

```swift
// BEFORE (AppViewModel.swift:13,18,19,48,49)
@Published var sourceImage: CIImage?
@Published var selectedLUT: CubeLUT?
@Published var processedImage: CIImage?
private let processor = ImageProcessor.shared
private var previewTask: Task<Void, Never>?

// AFTER
@Published var document = EditDocument()        // Sendable value (the look)
private(set) var source: ImageSource?           // Sendable descriptor (URL + kind + extent)
@Published var previewNSImage: NSImage?         // unchanged (built on MainActor from CGImage)
@Published var originalPreviewNSImage: NSImage?
private var renderTask: Task<Void, Never>?      // replaces previewTask
private var lastRenderedDoc: EditDocument?      // for debounce/coalescing
```

`AppViewModel` stays `@MainActor`. It no longer stores `processor`. It no longer holds any `CIImage`. The only non-Sendable thing it holds is `NSImage` (fine — main-actor only, never crosses out).

`openImage` (AppViewModel.swift:72-100) — build an `ImageSource` instead of decoding to a stored `CIImage`. Native extent is obtained cheaply via `CGImageSource` properties (no full decode) or, for the data path, by a one-off decode on the actor:

```swift
func openImage(url: URL) {
    isLoading = true
    let kind: ImageSource.Kind = ImageProcessorFormats.isRAW(url) ? .raw : .standard
    Task {
        // size probe is cheap + Sendable-safe (CGImageSource on the actor or a free fn returning CGSize)
        let extent = await RenderEngine.shared.nativeExtent(url: url, kind: kind) ?? .zero
        self.source = ImageSource(url: url, kind: kind, nativeExtent: extent)
        self.sourceName = url.lastPathComponent
        self.sourceSize = extent
        self.isLoading = false
        await renderAll()              // renders preview + original via the actor
    }
}
```
(Add `func nativeExtent(url:kind:) -> CGSize?` to the actor, or a free `Sendable` helper that uses `CGImageSourceCopyPropertiesAtIndex`. Either is Sendable-clean because it returns a `CGSize`.)

`applyLUT()` → `scheduleRender()` (replaces AppViewModel.swift:224-245):

```swift
// BEFORE — heavy work inherits @MainActor; captures self; CIImage stored
previewTask?.cancel()
previewTask = Task {                     // @MainActor-inherited
    guard let result = lut.apply(to: source) else { ... }
    guard !Task.isCancelled else { return }
    self.processedImage = result
    self.updatePreview(result)
}

// AFTER — off-actor render; only Sendable values cross; CGImage back
private func scheduleRender() {
    guard let source else { return }
    renderTask?.cancel()
    let doc = document                    // value copy — Sendable, captured by value
    let space = WorkingSpace.current
    let maxSize = maxPreview
    renderTask = Task { [weak self] in    // NOT detached; suspends on await, body runs on MainActor between awaits
        // debounce: coalesce rapid slider edits
        try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 16_000_000)   // ~1 frame; tune
        if Task.isCancelled { return }
        let cg = await RenderEngine.shared.renderPreview(
            source: source, document: doc, maxSize: maxSize, space: space)
        if Task.isCancelled { return }
        guard let self, let cg else { return }
        self.previewNSImage = NSImage(cgImage: cg, size: cg.sizeCG)   // on MainActor
    }
}
```
Key points: (a) `doc`/`source`/`space`/`maxSize` are all `Sendable`, captured by value — no `self` crosses into the actor call, only into the main-actor continuation via `[weak self]`. (b) The `Task` is intentionally NON-detached so the `self.previewNSImage = ...` write lands on `@MainActor` without an explicit `MainActor.run`. (c) Cancellation now actually saves GPU work because the actor hasn't started until `await`, and you can additionally have the actor check `Task.isCancelled` between pipeline stages if you thread cancellation in. (d) Call `scheduleRender()` from a `didSet` on `document` or from `selectLUT`/develop-slider setters; the `EditDocument: Equatable` lets you early-out if `document == lastRenderedDoc`.

`showOriginal(_:)` (AppViewModel.swift:260-269) — instead of re-rendering from `processedImage`, render an `EditDocument` with `lut.intensity = 0` (cheap, same path) OR cache the two CGImages. Either way it goes through `RenderEngine.renderPreview`, never touching a stored CIImage.

`exportDialog()` (AppViewModel.swift:277-312) — the headline fix:

```swift
// BEFORE — illegal under strict concurrency
Task.detached { [processor, exportFormat] in            // ① non-Sendable processor captured
    do {
        try processor.export(imageToExport, to: url, format: exportFormat)  // ② CIImage captured
        await MainActor.run { self.isExporting = false; ... }               // ③ self captured
    } catch { await MainActor.run { self.isExporting = false; ... } }
}

// AFTER — only Sendable values cross; no processor/self/CIImage capture
guard let source else { statusMessage = "Open an image first"; return }
let doc = document; let fmt = exportFormat; let space = WorkingSpace.current
isExporting = true; statusMessage = "Exporting…"
Task {                                                   // NON-detached: continuation is on MainActor
    do {
        try await RenderEngine.shared.export(
            source: source, document: doc, to: url, format: fmt, space: space)
        self.isExporting = false                         // MainActor — no MainActor.run needed
        self.statusMessage = "Exported: \(url.lastPathComponent)"
    } catch {
        self.isExporting = false
        self.statusMessage = "Export failed: \(error.localizedDescription)"
    }
}
```
`source`, `doc`, `fmt` (`ExportFormat` made `Sendable`), `space`, `url` are all Sendable. The export now RE-RENDERS the full-res image from the same document (preview/export parity) instead of reusing a baked `processedImage` — which is also why `processedImage` is deleted.

`deriveRecipe(rawURL:jpgURL:)` (AppViewModel.swift:328-400) — keep `Task.detached` (derive is a genuinely separate batch job that must not contend with the interactive actor), but make every captured thing Sendable and remove the `self` capture from the detached body:

```swift
// BEFORE — self captured in detached body and in progress closure
Task.detached {
    let result = try RecipeExtractor.derive(rawURL: rawURL, jpgURL: jpgURL,
        progress: { p, s in Task { @MainActor in self.deriveProgress = p; self.deriveStage = s } }) // self
    await MainActor.run { self.derivedLUT = lut; ...; self.selectLUT(lut) }                          // self
}

// AFTER — capture only Sendable values; hop back via an isolated continuation, not captured self
let raw = rawURL, jpg = jpgURL
Task {                                            // non-detached outer; we still get off-main via the await
    // progress is @Sendable and only touches MainActor via an explicit hop with a captured weak self
    let progress: @Sendable (Double, String) -> Void = { p, s in
        Task { @MainActor [weak self] in self?.deriveProgress = p; self?.deriveStage = s }
    }
    do {
        let result = try await Task.detached { 
            try RecipeExtractor.derive(rawURL: raw, jpgURL: jpg, progress: progress)
        }.value                                    // result is Sendable DeriveResult
        // back on MainActor automatically (outer Task is @MainActor-affine via [weak self] continuation):
        // build CubeLUT, register with RenderEngine, set document.lut.lutID
        let id = LUTID(raw: UUID())
        let lut = CubeLUT(cube: result.cube, size: result.size, name: cubeName,
                          category: "Derived", sourceURL: scratchURL)   // CubeLUT now Sendable
        await RenderEngine.shared.register(lut, for: id)
        self.derivedLUT = lut
        self.derivedReport = result.report
        self.document.lut.lutID = id              // triggers scheduleRender via didSet
        self.isDeriving = false
    } catch {
        self.isDeriving = false; self.statusMessage = "Derive failed: \(error.localizedDescription)"
    }
}
```
Requirements this imposes on `RecipeExtractor` (Section 6): `DeriveResult` (`cube: [SIMD3<Float>]`, `size: Int`, `report: RecipeReport`) must be `Sendable`; the `progress` parameter type must be `@Sendable (Double, String) -> Void`; `derive` stays `static` and uses its OWN `CIContext`. The ONE genuinely new wiring step is `RenderEngine.register(lut, for: id)` + setting `document.lut.lutID` so the live preview resolves the derived LUT through the registry.

---

### 5. `CubeLUT` (MODIFIED) — make it Sendable + registry-ready

```swift
struct CubeLUT: Identifiable, Hashable, Sendable {     // add Sendable
    let id: String
    let lutID: LUTID                                     // NEW stable identity (derive from id or UUID)
    let name: String
    let category: String
    let url: URL
    let size: Int
    private let tableData: Data                          // Data is Sendable ✓
    // ...
}
```
All stored properties (`String`, `URL`, `Int`, `Data`, `LUTID`) are `Sendable`, so the conformance is synthesized — no `@unchecked`. (`SIMD3<Float>` arrays in the initializers are locals, not stored, so they don't affect conformance.) `makeFilter(colorSpace:)` and `applyWithIntensity(to:intensity:colorSpace:)` return/handle `CIImage`/`CIFilter` and therefore must be called only inside the actor — they are, from `RenderPipeline.buildImage`. Add a per-color-space filter cache (owned by the color/intensity components); from a concurrency view, ensure any such cache is either actor-isolated state or computed without mutation, so a `Sendable` `CubeLUT` value stays immutable. If a mutable cache is desired, store it on the actor (`lutRegistry` side table), NOT on the `CubeLUT` value, to keep `CubeLUT` a clean `Sendable` value.

---

### 6. `ImageProcessor` dissolution (MODIFIED, ideally renamed/removed)

- DELETE GPU members: `context` (line 13), `renderPreview` (94), `renderToNSImage` (141), `export` (174), `generateThumbnail`×2 (119/125), `thumbnailFromSource` (130) → all rehomed on `RenderEngine`.
- KEEP as static/value-only vocabulary, rehomed into an enum namespace (e.g. `enum ImageProcessorFormats`) so nothing references a non-Sendable instance: `rawExtensions`, `standardExtensions`, `supportedExtensions`, `supportedTypes`, and `static func isRAW(_ url: URL) -> Bool`. These are `static let` constants — already concurrency-safe.
- KEEP `static func developRAWNeutral(at:)` (line 74) verbatim — it is the single neutral-baseline definition shared by the (now actor-side) RAW develop and by `RecipeExtractor`. It returns a `CIImage`, so its callers must be inside an isolation domain: `RenderPipeline` (actor) and `RecipeExtractor` (its own context). Document that it must not be called from `@MainActor`.
- PROMOTE `ExportFormat` to a top-level `enum ExportFormat: String, CaseIterable, Identifiable, Sendable` (currently nested `ImageProcessor.ExportFormat`, AppViewModel.swift:29 references it). Update that reference to the new name.
- MARK `enum ImageError: LocalizedError, Sendable` (line 219) — its cases carry only `String`, so `Sendable` is free; needed because it is thrown across the `await` from the actor's `export`.

After this, there is NO non-Sendable singleton anywhere in the target.

---

### 7. `ImageCollection` + `FilmstripView` (MODIFIED)

- `ImageCollection` (subsystem map: `Task.detached` at ~:40 hitting `ImageProcessor.shared`): change to `let cg = await RenderEngine.shared.thumbnail(url: u, maxPixelSize: n)` and assign the resulting `NSImage` on `@MainActor`. Ensure `ImageItem` (and any captured collection element) is `Sendable` — if it stores `Data`/`URL`/`String` it is; if it stores `NSImage`, split the stored thumbnail so the NSImage is written on the main actor only and the detached job carries just `URL`/`Data`/index (all Sendable). If `ImageCollection` is an `ObservableObject` updated from a detached task, route mutations through `@MainActor` (it likely already does via the existing `MainActor` hop; verify there is no `self` capture leaking a non-Sendable field).
- `FilmstripView` (FilmstripView.swift ~:88, `processor.generateThumbnail`): replace with `Task { let cg = await RenderEngine.shared.thumbnail(url:maxPixelSize:); await MainActor.run { self.thumb = cg.map { NSImage(cgImage: $0, size: ...) } } }` or, preferably, drive it through `ImageCollection` so the view stays declarative. The view must not capture or call the old singleton.

---

### 8. Build-setting rollout (MODIFIED project)

1. Land all the above with `SWIFT_VERSION = 5` and **`SWIFT_STRICT_CONCURRENCY = minimal`** (current default) — confirm it still compiles/runs (behavioral parity).
2. Flip to `SWIFT_STRICT_CONCURRENCY = targeted`, fix any warnings (should be none in the touched files).
3. Flip to `SWIFT_STRICT_CONCURRENCY = complete`; resolve residual warnings (e.g. if the compiler refuses bare `CGImage` returns, apply the `RenderedImage` `@unchecked Sendable` wrapper noted in §1 — last resort only).
4. Finally set `SWIFT_VERSION = 6`. Each step is a separate commit so a regression is bisectable.

This staged flip is the verification ladder: the design's claim ("flipping strict concurrency is a non-event") is *proven* by getting clean builds at each rung, not asserted.

---

### Before/after capture-safety summary (the four hot crossings)

| Site | Before (illegal/heavy) | After (Sendable-clean) |
|---|---|---|
| `exportDialog` AppViewModel.swift:297 | `Task.detached { [processor, exportFormat] … self … imageToExport:CIImage }` | `Task { try await RenderEngine.shared.export(source:document:to:format:space:) }`; captures `source, doc, fmt, space, url` (all Sendable) |
| `applyLUT` AppViewModel.swift:236 | `Task { lut.apply(to:source) }` on @MainActor (heavy) | `Task { await RenderEngine.shared.renderPreview(...) }`; CIImage work off-main, only `doc/source/space/maxSize` cross |
| `deriveRecipe` AppViewModel.swift:343 | `Task.detached { … self … progress{ self … } }` | `Task.detached { RecipeExtractor.derive(raw,jpg,@Sendable progress) }.value`; result `Sendable`; `self` only via `[weak self]` MainActor continuation |
| `ImageCollection`/`FilmstripView` thumbnails | `Task.detached` → `ImageProcessor.shared.generateThumbnail` | `await RenderEngine.shared.thumbnail(url:)` → CGImage → NSImage on MainActor |

**Testing:** BUILD-LEVEL (the primary proof for this component):
- Compile gate ladder: build green at SWIFT_STRICT_CONCURRENCY = minimal → targeted → complete, then SWIFT_VERSION 6. The component is "done" only when `xcodebuild -scheme LUTzy -configuration Debug SWIFT_STRICT_CONCURRENCY=complete SWIFT_VERSION=6 build` produces zero data-race/Sendable warnings in the touched files. Capture the warning count at each rung.
- Grep assertions after refactor: `grep -rn "ImageProcessor.shared" LUTzy/` returns ONLY non-GPU usages (formats/developRAWNeutral) or nothing; `grep -rn "MainActor.run" LUTzy/ViewModels` returns nothing (replaced by non-detached Tasks); `grep -rn "@unchecked Sendable" LUTzy/` returns nothing (or only the one documented CGImage wrapper if §1 fallback was needed); `grep -rn "processedImage\|sourceImage\b" LUTzy/` returns nothing.
- Confirm `EditDocument`, `RAWDevelopSettings`, `AdjustmentNode`, `LUTSettings`, `LUTID`, `ImageSource`, `RenderScale`, `WorkingSpace`, `ExportFormat`, `CubeLUT`, `ImageError`, `RecipeExtractor.DeriveResult` all conform to `Sendable` — add a compile-time test file with `func _assertSendable<T: Sendable>(_ t: T.Type) {}` calling each, so any future field that breaks conformance fails the build.

CONCURRENCY-BEHAVIOR (manual + TSan):
- Run the app under Thread Sanitizer (Xcode scheme → Diagnostics → Thread Sanitizer). Exercise: open RAW, drag a develop/intensity slider rapidly (stress the debounced renderTask + actor serialization), A/B Space-hold, switch LUTs fast, export while a preview render is in flight, derive a LUT while scrubbing. TSan must report zero races on the CIContext.
- Cancellation: drag intensity slider continuously, confirm only the final render's NSImage lands (add a temporary os_signpost/print of render generation count; rapid edits should NOT produce N full renders — debounce + cancel should coalesce). Verify renderTask?.cancel() before each new schedule.
- Export-while-rendering: trigger export, immediately scrub preview; both complete, no crash, no torn image (actor serializes). Exported file opens and matches the on-screen preview (parity — shared buildImage).
- Derive isolation: start a derive on a large pair, scrub the preview meanwhile; UI stays responsive, derive completes, derived LUT auto-applies (document.lut.lutID set + RenderEngine.register fired). Confirm via a breakpoint that RecipeExtractor.derive runs on a non-RenderEngine context (its own makeContext) and that developRAWNeutral is used for its baseline (neutral contract intact).
- Main-thread responsiveness: with Time Profiler, confirm CIContext.createCGImage / filter-graph build no longer appears on the main thread during preview (it did before, via the @MainActor-inherited previewTask).

REGRESSION / PARITY:
- Byte-equivalence of neutral baseline: render a RAW with EditDocument() (all-neutral) through the new path and compare to the OLD loadRAW→renderPreview output (same scale) — must be visually identical (developRAWNeutral unchanged).
- Derived-LUT correctness unaffected: derive the same (RAW,JPG) pair before and after the refactor; the written .cube files must be identical (RecipeExtractor logic untouched; only its result-type Sendability and call site changed).
- Thumbnails: filmstrip populates identically after moving generateThumbnail to the actor (same CGImageSource options).

EDGE CASES:
- Photos data path (openImage(data:name:)): no URL, so ImageSource must support a data-backed source OR the data path decodes once on the actor and caches an extent; verify export from a Photos import still works (today it can export the baked processedImage — after refactor it must re-render from the data source; ensure ImageSource models this or a CIImage cache is held inside the actor keyed by an id). FLAG: this is the one place the URL-only ImageSource needs a data variant — confirm during implementation.
- WorkingSpace.displayP3 path is unreachable from UI (current = .sRGB) but unit-test that WorkingSpace.displayP3.cgColorSpace force-unwrap does not crash, since it shares the accessor.

**Reviewer-flagged issues:**
- WRONG TYPE NAME (will not compile as written): The spec repeatedly references `RecipeExtractor.DeriveResult` (in filesToChange, newTypes, and the deriveRecipe rewrite via `result.report`/`result.cube`/`result.size`). The real type is `RecipeExtractor.Result` (nested struct, RecipeExtractor.swift:65-69) holding `cube: [SIMD3<Float>]`, `size: Int`, `report: RecipeReport`. The 'make DeriveResult Sendable' instruction must be retargeted to `RecipeExtractor.Result`.
- MISSING REQUIRED Sendable CONFORMANCE: For `RecipeExtractor.Result` to cross the `Task.detached{...}.value` boundary it must be Sendable, which transitively requires `RecipeReport` AND `RecipeReport.CameraInfo` to be Sendable. Both are currently only `Equatable` (RecipeReport.swift:5,20) and appear NOWHERE in the spec's filesToChange/newTypes. They are plain structs of Sendable members (Strings/Floats/Int + the `alignmentShift: (dx:Int,dy:Int)` tuple, which is Sendable), so adding `: Sendable` is trivial — but it is an unlisted, load-bearing change. RecipeReport.swift must be added to filesToChange.
- FALSE PREMISE — FilmstripView needs NO change: The spec's filesToChange and risks both claim `FilmstripView.swift ~:88, processor.generateThumbnail`. The real FilmstripView.swift (74 lines, read in full) contains ZERO references to ImageProcessor or generateThumbnail; it only renders `item.thumbnail` from the collection. The entire 'MODIFIED FilmstripView' task and its testing note are based on a non-existent call site (the spec hedged it from a subsystem map). Thumbnail generation lives only in ImageCollection.
- MISSED SECOND THUMBNAIL CALL SITE: ImageCollection has TWO ImageProcessor.shared.generateThumbnail touch points, not one. The spec only addresses the `Task.detached` path in `generateThumbnails()` (ImageCollection.swift:100-102). It misses `addFromData(_:)` (ImageCollection.swift:61-66) which calls `processor.generateThumbnail(from: item.data)` SYNCHRONOUSLY on the @MainActor. After the singleton's GPU duties move to the actor, this synchronous call must also become `await RenderEngine.shared.thumbnail(data:)`, forcing addFromData async or into a Task — unaddressed.
- REGISTRY-BY-ID INSTABILITY (correctness bug the concurrency design inherits): The spec resolves `EditDocument.lut.lutID` through `RenderEngine.lutRegistry`, and CubeLUT gets a new `lutID` 'derived from id or UUID'. But LUTLibrary.scan (LUTLibrary.swift:57-96) REBUILDS all CubeLUT values on every folder scan, and saveDerivedLUT() (AppViewModel.swift:427-429) triggers a re-scan. If lutID is a fresh UUID per init, the registry key for a given file changes across scans and any persisted/active `document.lut.lutID` stops resolving. lutID must be a STABLE function of the file path (e.g. hashed from `id`/url.path) for file-backed LUTs, or document resolution must also consult LUTLibrary by path — neither is specified.
- deriveRecipe REWRITE DROPS SCRATCH-FILE BOOKKEEPING (regression): The real deriveRecipe writes a scratch `.cube` via `CubeLUT.write(...)` (AppViewModel.swift:363-368) and stores `derivedScratchURL` (set at :381), which `saveDerivedLUT()` (AppViewModel.swift:404-434) reads to copy the file on Save. The spec's 'AFTER' deriveRecipe sketch registers the LUT and sets `document.lut.lutID` but omits both the `CubeLUT.write` step and setting `derivedScratchURL`, which would break the Save-to-LUT-folder feature. The concurrency rewrite must preserve this surrounding bookkeeping.
- Photos data path is a HARD BLOCKER, not just an open question: openImage(data:name:) (AppViewModel.swift:116-139) creates a URL-less source (`sourceURL = nil`), and today export uses the baked `processedImage` (AppViewModel.swift:283). The spec deletes `processedImage` and makes export re-render from a URL-only `ImageSource`, which breaks export AND any re-render for Photos imports. The spec lists this under openQuestions/risks, but since openImage(data:) is a shipped code path, the data-backed ImageSource (`enum Backing { case url(URL); case data(Data) }`) is mandatory for this component to compile to a working app, not optional.
- INCOMPLETE ExportFormat reference migration: Promoting `ImageProcessor.ExportFormat` to top-level must update `@Published var exportFormat: ImageProcessor.ExportFormat` (AppViewModel.swift:29) AND `ImageProcessor.ExportFormat.allCases` in ContentView.swift:79 (toolbar Picker). The spec only cites AppViewModel:29 and the ContentView reference is exactly the 'grep before deleting' miss it warns about elsewhere.

**Open questions:**
- ImageSource is URL-only, but openImage(data:name:) (AppViewModel.swift:116) has NO URL (Photos imports). The actor needs a data-backed source variant OR an internal CIImage cache keyed by a Sendable id so export can re-render. Decide: add `enum ImageSource.Backing { case url(URL); case data(Data) }` (Data is Sendable) vs. cache the decoded CIImage inside RenderEngine keyed by LUTID-style token. The data-backed enum is simpler and keeps everything Sendable; recommend that.
- Does the strict-concurrency compiler accept a bare `CGImage?` return across the actor boundary? Apple's annotated CoreGraphics headers generally treat CGImage as Sendable, but if `complete` checking flags it, we fall back to `struct RenderedImage: @unchecked Sendable { let cg: CGImage }`. Confirm empirically at the `complete` rung before finalizing the actor's public signatures.
- previewNSImage/originalPreviewNSImage remain @Published NSImage on @MainActor. NSImage is not Sendable but never crosses out of the main actor in the new design — confirm no view/code path passes them into a Task/actor. (Today nothing does; just guard against regressions.)
- RecipeExtractor.derive currently returns a result type and takes a progress closure; verify the real result type and closure signature when the file becomes readable, then make the result Sendable and the closure @Sendable. The map says it's `static func derive(rawURL:jpgURL:progress:)` returning {cube,size,report}; I could not re-open the file (transient tool failures) to confirm exact property names/optionality — validate against source before coding the DeriveResult: Sendable conformance.
- Debounce interval (sketched at ~16ms) for scheduleRender needs tuning for power-user slider drags vs. perceived latency; also decide whether to additionally thread Task cancellation INTO the actor (check Task.isCancelled between pipeline stages) for finer-grained GPU work savings, or rely on the pre-await coalescing alone.
- CubeLUT filter cache (perf, from intensity/color components) must not introduce mutable state on the Sendable CubeLUT value. Decide whether the per-color-space CIFilter cache lives on the actor (recommended — keeps CubeLUT immutable) or is recomputed per render; this is a concurrency-correctness decision even though perf-motivated.
- Should ImageProcessor be fully deleted (rehoming formats to `enum ImageProcessorFormats`, ExportFormat/ImageError to top level) or left as a thin static-only caseless enum? Deletion is cleanest for proving 'no non-Sendable singleton remains' but touches more references (e.g. AppViewModel.swift:29,105). Confirm scope with maintainer.

**Risks:**
- I could not re-read RecipeExtractor.swift, ImageCollection.swift, or FilmstripView.swift directly (repeated transient tool failures); their concurrency touch-points are taken from the line-cited subsystem maps, which the maps themselves warn can have name drift. Before coding, re-open these three files and confirm: RecipeExtractor.derive's exact signature/result type, ImageCollection's Task.detached thumbnail site + whether ImageItem stores NSImage (non-Sendable), and FilmstripView's generateThumbnail call. The actor.thumbnail signatures and the DeriveResult: Sendable change depend on these.
- Behavioral change baked into the concurrency fix: export now RE-RENDERS full-res from EditDocument instead of reusing the baked processedImage. This is correct for parity but means deleting processedImage; if any other code reads processedImage it must be migrated. Risk of a missed reference — grep before deleting.
- Actor serialization could increase preview latency under a flood of slider events if the debounce is too short and renders queue behind a slow full-res export on the SAME actor. Mitigation: keep export and preview on the same actor (correctness) but ensure preview is downscaled-early so it is cheap; consider a separate lower-priority Task for export. Validate under load.
- The Photos data path (openImage(data:)) has no URL and breaks the URL-only ImageSource; if not handled, export/derive from a Photos import regresses. Must add a data-backed ImageSource or an actor-side CIImage cache (see open questions).
- developRAWNeutral(at:) returns a CIImage and is static on ImageProcessor; once GPU duties move to the actor, callers must remain inside an isolation domain (RenderPipeline actor-side, RecipeExtractor own-context). If a future caller invokes it from @MainActor it reintroduces heavy work on the main thread — add a doc comment and consider a debug assertion that it isn't called on MainActor.
- If CGImage is rejected as Sendable under complete checking, the @unchecked Sendable wrapper reintroduces an (audited) escape hatch, weakening the 'zero @unchecked' guarantee. Acceptable but should be documented and centralized to one type.
- Pre-existing PreviewView FIXME (undefined `preview` in the graded branch, per maps) is OUT OF SCOPE here but sits in the same render-display path; flag separately so it isn't conflated with this refactor's correctness.
- Two sources of truth for 'what is an image' (ImageProcessor format sets vs ImageCollection.isImageFile) — ImageSource.kind routing assumes ONE set. If they disagree, RAW vs standard misclassification feeds the wrong branch of buildImage. Consolidate onto ImageProcessorFormats.isRAW during this change to avoid a latent inconsistency.

### Export + color-management seam

**Verdict:** major-issues (concurrency FLAG · color OK · preview/export OK)

**Summary.** Spec for moving export off the cached `processedImage` blob and onto the same `RenderPipeline.buildImage(...)` node graph used by preview, evaluated at full resolution inside `actor RenderEngine`; collapsing the 3 hardcoded `CGColorSpace.sRGB` literals (CubeLUT.makeFilter:135, ImageProcessor.export:163, the implicit preview-raster site ImageProcessor.swift:92) plus the LUT-interp/output coupling into ONE `WorkingSpace` value threaded through both encode and LUT-interpolation; and adding EXIF/orientation/ICC preservation on export (currently zero metadata is written) via a per-format options dictionary built from the source file's `CGImageSource` properties. TIFF16 / JPEG / PNG are preserved, with bit depth and quality promoted into a Sendable `ExportRequest` descriptor so the JPEG-quality wiring gap (exportDialog never passes quality, so it is always 0.95) is closed. RecipeExtractor's own sRGB + own CIContext stay physically decoupled (its derived LUT is fit and applied in sRGB; it is NOT routed through WorkingSpace in v1). All boundary-crossing types are Sendable value types for Swift 6.

**Files to change:**
- NEW /Users/tim/Documents/Claude/Projects/LUTzy/LUTzy-macOS/LUTzy/Models/WorkingSpace.swift — the single color-space seam (enum WorkingSpace + cgColorSpace accessor + static current).
- NEW /Users/tim/Documents/Claude/Projects/LUTzy/LUTzy-macOS/LUTzy/Models/ExportRequest.swift — Sendable export descriptor (format + bit depth + quality + metadata policy) and the ExportFormat enum MOVED here from ImageProcessor.
- NEW /Users/tim/Documents/Claude/Projects/LUTzy/LUTzy-macOS/LUTzy/Models/ExportMetadata.swift — reads EXIF/TIFF/IPTC/GPS/orientation from the SOURCE file via CGImageSource and builds the per-format options dict (incl. ICC profile data) for the CIContext encoders.

Keep/relocate ExportFormat, ImageError. (If RenderEngine lands in a new file, ImageProcessor's export+context duties are dissolved per the binding decision; this spec only owns the export+colorspace slice of that dissolution.)
- MODIFIED /Users/tim/Documents/Claude/Projects/LUTzy/LUTzy-macOS/LUTzy/Models/CubeLUT.swift — makeFilter() (line ~131) takes a `colorSpace: CGColorSpace` param instead of constructing sRGB inline at line 135; cache the built CIFilter keyed by colorSpace name (fixes per-apply realloc at line 141).

Included so a future P3 switch has a tracked checkpoint.
