### 5. PreviewView edits (state migration only — it already compiles)

```swift
// line 14:  if viewModel.sourceImage != nil      ->  if viewModel.source != nil
// line 15:  isSideBySide && viewModel.selectedLUT != nil  ->  isSideBySide && viewModel.document.lut.lutID != nil
// line 54:  label: viewModel.selectedLUT?.name ?? "LUT"   ->  label: viewModel.currentLUT?.name ?? "LUT"
// line 72,91: .animation(..., value: viewModel.selectedLUT)  ->  value: viewModel.document.lut.lutID
// singleView (line 85): pick buffer for A/B:
let img = viewModel.isShowingOriginal ? viewModel.originalPreviewNSImage : viewModel.previewNSImage
if let img { Image(nsImage: img).resizable().aspectRatio(contentMode: .fit) ... }
// badges line 94/106: viewModel.selectedLUT  ->  viewModel.currentLUT (or document.lut.lutID != nil)
```
`currentLUT` is the VM mirror (a `CubeLUT?` updated in `selectLUT`, used only for display/index math). No undefined-symbol fix is needed; the brief's FIXME does not exist in this file.

---

### 6. Before/after user flow
- BEFORE: pick a LUT in the sidebar → `applyLUT()` builds one CIFilter at 100% inside a `Task {}` that **inherits @MainActor** (filter graph runs on the main thread) → `processedImage` → `renderPreview`. No develop, no intensity, no inspector. Export reuses `processedImage ?? sourceImage`.
- AFTER: any inspector control mutates `document` → `.removeDuplicates().debounce(80ms)` → `scheduleRender` → `await RenderEngine.renderPreview` **off the main actor** → `previewNSImage`. Export renders the identical `document` at full res via the same actor + same `WorkingSpace`. Space-hold and side-by-side compare against the cached neutral `originalPreviewNSImage` (no re-render).

**Testing:** ## 7. Test target setup (SPM, NOT Xcode — this is the big correction)

There is no `.xcodeproj`; `Package.swift` has a single `.executableTarget`. Two facts drive the setup:

1. **You cannot `@testable import` an executable target** whose module contains `@main` (`LUTzyApp`). The fix: refactor into a library + thin executable:
```swift
// Package.swift — AFTER
let package = Package(
    name: "LUTzy",
    platforms: [.macOS(.v14)],
    targets: [
        .target(name: "LUTzyKit", path: "LUTzy",
                exclude: ["LUTzyApp.swift"]),              // everything EXCEPT @main
        .executableTarget(name: "LUTzy", dependencies: ["LUTzyKit"],
                path: "Sources/LUTzyApp"),                 // move LUTzyApp.swift here, `import LUTzyKit`
        .testTarget(name: "LUTzyTests", dependencies: ["LUTzyKit"],
                path: "Tests/LUTzyTests",
                resources: [.copy("Fixtures")]),
    ])
```
   Moving `@main` requires `LUTzyApp.swift` to `import LUTzyKit` and the app types to be reachable. Minimal-churn alternative if the team rejects a second target: keep one `.executableTarget` and add `.testTarget` that lists the few `.swift` files it needs via a shared sources path — but SPM does not allow a test target to depend on an executable's symbols, so the library split is the correct, standard approach. Flag this as a required refactor in the PR description.
2. **Access control**: with a real module boundary, `@testable import LUTzyKit` exposes `internal` symbols, so no `public` changes are needed *for tests*. BUT the thin executable needs `ContentView`/`AppViewModel` to be at least `public` (or keep `ContentView` in the Kit and only `LUTzyApp` in the executable — preferred: `LUTzyApp` is the only file in the executable, it references `ContentView()` which must therefore be `public`). Spec choice: make `ContentView`, `AppViewModel`, and the menu `Notification.Name`s `public`; everything else stays `internal` and is reached via `@testable`.

Run: `swift test` (CLI) or Xcode's generated scheme. CI: `swift test --parallel`.

## TestSupport.swift
```swift
import CoreImage; import CoreGraphics; @testable import LUTzyKit; import XCTest
func makeGradientCIImage(_ size: CGSize = .init(width: 64, height: 64)) -> CIImage {
    let g = CIFilter.smoothLinearGradient()
    g.point0 = .zero; g.point1 = CGPoint(x: size.width, y: size.height)
    g.color0 = .black; g.color1 = .white
    return g.outputImage!.cropped(to: CGRect(origin: .zero, size: size))
}
func tinyCubeText(size: Int, _ f: (SIMD3<Float>) -> SIMD3<Float>) -> String {
    // emit TITLE/LUT_3D_SIZE/DOMAIN + size^3 rows, R fastest, matching CubeLUT parser
}
func writeTemp(_ text: String, ext: String = "cube") -> URL { /* temporaryDirectory + UUID */ }
let testContext = CIContext(options: [.useSoftwareRenderer: true])   // deterministic in CI
func renderRGBA8(_ ci: CIImage, _ rect: CGRect, _ cs: CGColorSpace = WorkingSpace.sRGB.cgColorSpace) -> [UInt8] {
    var b = [UInt8](repeating: 0, count: Int(rect.width)*Int(rect.height)*4)
    b.withUnsafeMutableBytes { testContext.render(ci, toBitmap: $0.baseAddress!,
        rowBytes: Int(rect.width)*4, bounds: rect, format: .RGBA8, colorSpace: cs) }
    return b
}
func maxAbsDiff(_ a: [UInt8], _ b: [UInt8]) -> Int { zip(a,b).map { abs(Int($0)-Int($1)) }.max() ?? 0 }
func assertClose(_ a: [UInt8], _ b: [UInt8], tol: Int = 2, _ msg: String = "", file: StaticString = #filePath, line: UInt = #line) {
    XCTAssertLessThanOrEqual(maxAbsDiff(a,b), tol, msg, file: file, line: line)
}
func fixture(_ name: String, _ ext: String) -> URL? { Bundle.module.url(forResource: name, withExtension: ext, subdirectory: "Fixtures") }
```
Use `.useSoftwareRenderer: true` for the **test** context so pixel comparisons are deterministic across CI machines/GPUs (the app keeps Metal). RAW fixtures live under `Tests/LUTzyTests/Fixtures/` and are reached via `Bundle.module`; every RAW/derive test does `guard let url = fixture("sample","dng") else { throw XCTSkip("no RAW fixture") }`.

## A) Cube round-trip (CubeLUTTests.swift)
- `testParseWriteParseIdentity`: write `tinyCubeText(size: 17, warp)` to disk → `CubeLUT(url:)` → `CubeLUT.write(cube:size:title:to:)` (reconstruct cube array from the parsed filter is awkward since `tableData` is private; instead compare the two on-disk texts after a normalize, OR add an `internal var debugCube: [SIMD3<Float>]` test hook). Assert `size` equal and the re-emitted rows match within 1e-5.
- `testParseRejectsBadSize`: `LUT_3D_SIZE` absent → `LUTError.invalidFormat`; wrong row count → `.invalidFormat("Expected … got …")` (matches the real messages at CubeLUT.swift:97,101).
- `testInMemoryInitPacking`: `CubeLUT(cube:size:name:)` with a known size-2 cube, apply to a 1x1 CIImage of a known color via `makeFilter`(new colorSpace param), render, and assert the output equals the expected mapped value (proves RGBA float packing + R-fastest order feeding `inputCubeData`).

## B) Node-order correctness (AdjustmentNodeTests.swift)
- `testOrderMatters`: render the gradient through `[.exposure(ev: 1), .colorControls(brightness:0,contrast:2,saturation:1)]` vs the reversed array (build via `RenderPipeline.buildImage(..., scale:.full)` with a standard `ImageSource` from a written PNG, OR call `AdjustmentNode.apply` fold directly on a CIImage to avoid file I/O). `XCTAssertGreaterThan(maxAbsDiff(out1,out2), 6)` — order is observable.
- `testEmptyAdjustmentsNoOp`: `[]` fold ≈ source (`assertClose(tol:1)`).
- `testSingleNodeEqualsDirectFilter`: `.colorControls(0.1,1.2,1.3)` via fold ≈ `image.applyingFilter("CIColorControls", parameters: [kCIInputBrightnessKey:0.1, kCIInputContrastKey:1.2, kCIInputSaturationKey:1.3])`. Pins exact keys.
- `testMoveAdjustment` (pure VM): append three nodes, `moveAdjustment(2, by:-1)`, assert order; out-of-range delta is a no-op.

## C) Preview == export parity (RenderParityTests.swift)
- `testPreviewEqualsExportAtFullScale`: write the gradient to a temp PNG → `ImageSource(.standard)`. (a) `await RenderEngine.shared.renderPreview(source:document:maxSize: HUGE, space:.current)` so no downscale → CGImage A. (b) `try await engine.export(... to: tmpPNG, format:.png, space:.current)`, re-read via `CIImage(contentsOf:)` → CGImage B. `assertClose(renderRGBA8(A), renderRGBA8(B), tol: 2)` over the same rect. Use a non-trivial document (one adjustment + an in-memory LUT registered via `engine.register`). Proves both paths apply identical nodes + identical color space.
- `testPreviewColorSpaceIsExplicit`: `renderPreview(...)?.colorSpace?.name == WorkingSpace.current.cgColorSpace.name`. Guards the latent "preview omits colorSpace" mismatch (today `renderPreview` createCGImage passes no colorSpace at ImageProcessor.swift:109).
- `testNodesAreResolutionIndependent`: render preview at 1600x1200 cap and a full export; Lanczos-downsample the export to the preview size with the SAME transform; `assertClose(tol: 5)` (looser — resample differs). A resolution-dependent node would blow past tol.

## D/E) Identity pipeline + LUT intensity (IdentityPipelineTests.swift)
- `testNeutralDocumentNoOp` (standard image): `EditDocument()` on the gradient PNG ≈ decoded source (`tol:2`).
- `testIdentityLUTFullIntensityNoOp`: register `tinyCubeText(size:17, { $0 })`, `document.lut = LUTSettings(lutID:id, intensity:1)` ≈ source (`tol:2`). Locks `makeFilter(colorSpace:)` + identity table + the `intensity>=1` short-circuit.
- `testIntensityZeroEqualsSource`: invert LUT (`{ 1 - $0 }`), `intensity:0` ≈ source.
- `testIntensityOneEqualsGraded`: same LUT, `intensity:1` ≈ directly-graded image.
- `testIntensityHalfIsBetween`: pick a mid pixel; assert its value lies strictly between source and graded on each channel (monotone dissolve), proving `CIDissolveTransition time = 1 - intensity` + working-space blend wiring.

## F) Derive-baseline invariance (DeriveBaselineTests.swift) — the value-add guarantee
- `testDeriveIgnoresUserDevelop` (needs RAW+JPEG fixture, else XCTSkip): call `RecipeExtractor.derive(rawURL:jpgURL:)` once → cube1. Mutate an unrelated `AppViewModel().document.rawDevelop.exposure = 3` (proves no shared state). Call `derive` again → cube2. Assert `cube1.cube == cube2.cube` (SIMD3 array equality). Because `derive`'s signature takes only URLs + Options, this is also a compile-time guarantee; the test documents it.
- `testNeutralEqualsRAWDefaults` (RAW fixture, else XCTSkip): render `ImageProcessor.developRAWNeutral(at: url)!` to RGBA8, and render `RenderPipeline.buildImage(source: rawSource, document: EditDocument(), lut:nil, scale:.full, space:.sRGB)!` to RGBA8 over the same rect; `assertClose(tol: 2)`. Proves the develop path with `.neutral` reproduces today's `loadRAW`/derive baseline, so adding develop controls cannot silently shift derive's "from" space. (This is the contract that ties `RAWDevelopSettings.neutral` to `developRAWNeutral`.)
- `testDeriveSamplingSpaceStaysSRGB`: assert `RecipeExtractor.derive` output is unchanged when `WorkingSpace.current` is flipped to `.displayP3` (if a test seam allows overriding it; otherwise assert by inspection that `derive` constructs its own `CGColorSpace(name: CGColorSpace.sRGB)!` at RecipeExtractor.swift:81 and never reads `WorkingSpace`). Guards the documented "do not thread WorkingSpace into derive" rule.

## Manual / UI smoke checklist (PR)
- Open RAW → RAW Develop section appears; open JPEG → hidden.
- Drag Exposure → preview updates ~1 frame after the 80 ms debounce; no main-thread stall (render is off-actor).
- Click an inspector numeric field, type `v` and `[` → side-by-side does NOT toggle, image does NOT cycle; click canvas → single-key shortcuts work again. Repeat with the sidebar Search field.
- Hold Space → cached original; release → graded; no re-render flicker.
- Capability gating: a file without EDR → EDR rows absent; a file with sharpness support → Sharpness row present.
- Derive a LUT → it appears in the sidebar + the inspector Picker; selecting it sets intensity 100%; lowering intensity blends back toward the develop result.
- Toggle inspector from toolbar and from the menu command.

**Reviewer-flagged issues:**
- CGImage is NOT Sendable, which breaks the spec's headline concurrency claim. The pinned signature `RenderEngine.renderPreview(...) -> CGImage?` returns a value from `actor RenderEngine` to the @MainActor VM (scheduleRender/openImage wrap it as NSImage(cgImage:)). CGImage is a CoreFoundation type with no Sendable conformance. The spec explicitly states 'No @unchecked Sendable escape hatches are needed anywhere - every boundary crossing is provably safe' and bills CGImage-not-bytes as a refinement over Approach 3. Under Swift 6 strict concurrency this return WILL warn/error. It needs `-> sending CGImage?` or an @unchecked Sendable wrapper - i.e. the exact escape hatch the spec denies needing. This is the single biggest correctness gap because Swift-6 readiness is the stated reason for the whole actor design.
- RAWCapabilities.probeRAW reads `f.isDustRemovalSupported`, which does not exist on CIRAWFilter. Verified against the real SDK header (MacOSX26.5.sdk .../CIRAWFilter.h): the support flags are isLensCorrectionSupported, isLuminanceNoiseReductionSupported, isColorNoiseReductionSupported, isSharpnessSupported, isContrastSupported, isDetailSupported, isMoireReductionSupported, isLocalToneMapSupported, isHighlightRecoverySupported. There is NO isDustRemovalSupported. The spec asserts these are 'SDK-verified names' but this one is fabricated - the RAWCapabilities builder will not compile as written. (lensCorrectionSupported IS real, so that field is fine.)
- EDR properties are macOS 15+, but the app targets macOS 14 (Package.swift: .macOS(.v14)). Verified: `isEnableEDRModeAvailable` and `isEDRModeEnabled` are both NS_AVAILABLE(15_0,18_0); `extendedDynamicRangeAmount` is NS_AVAILABLE(15_0,18_0). The architecture's RAWDevelopSettings.apply gates only on the runtime flag `f.isEnableEDRModeAvailable` and the inspector gates the row on rawCaps.enableEDRAvailable, but neither wraps the symbol access in `if #available(macOS 15, *)`. On the v14 deployment target these property references fail to compile (or require availability guards) in probeRAW, apply(to:), and RAWDevelopSection. The spec/architecture treat EDR as a plain macOS-14 knob; it is not.
- ExportFormat ownership is internally contradictory. The pinned contract is `RenderEngine.export(... format: ImageProcessor.ExportFormat ...)` and ContentView keeps `ForEach(ImageProcessor.ExportFormat.allCases)` (real code: ContentView.swift:79), while the concurrency section says 'ImageProcessor is dissolved.' You cannot delete ImageProcessor and still reference ImageProcessor.ExportFormat as the export param type AND the toolbar Picker source AND the VM property type (AppViewModel.swift:29 `@Published var exportFormat: ImageProcessor.ExportFormat`). It must be lifted to a top-level enum with all three sites updated together; the spec only lists this as an open question while pinning the old nested type into the binding contract.
- CITemperatureAndTint adjustment node likely inverts the creative control. Verified the filter exposes only `neutral` (source white point) and `targetNeutral` (default [6500,0]). AdjustmentNode.temperatureTint sets ONLY inputTargetNeutral = CIVector(temp,tint) and leaves inputNeutral at default. Identity holds at (6500,0) so the seed is harmless, but as a creative slider, RAISING the target Kelvin makes the image COOLER (the filter shifts the image so that the target becomes the new neutral), which is backwards from every photo-editor 'temperature' slider, and the effect magnitude is relative to a fixed 6500 source. The slider range temp 2000-10000 will feel inverted to power users.
- No RAW preview-vs-export parity test, which is precisely the fragile path. testPreviewEqualsExportAtFullScale uses a standard PNG ImageSource; testNodesAreResolutionIndependent uses a standard image with tol 5. Both avoid the genuinely risky case: RAW preview uses CIRAWFilter.scaleFactor draft demosaic while RAW export uses full decode, so RAW preview != RAW export by construction. The spec acknowledges the draft-demosaic delta as an open question but ships zero tests guarding RAW develop wiring symmetry, so a regression where a develop knob is applied at preview but not export (or vice versa) passes CI.
- JPEG-quality baseline in the open questions is factually wrong. The spec claims today's JPEG export is 'effectively fixed at 0.95.' Real code (ImageProcessor.export, line 198) DOES pass `[kCGImageDestinationLossyCompressionQuality: quality]` with quality defaulting to 0.95, but exportDialog calls `processor.export(imageToExport, to: url, format: exportFormat)` WITHOUT passing quality - so it uses the function default 0.95. So 0.95 is actually correct by default-arg, but the brief elsewhere (and an earlier draft framing) muddles whether it is wired. Net: the architecture's RenderEngine.export(...quality:) is fine, but exportDialog in the spec hardcodes 0.95 while the real default path already is 0.95 - confirm the value is intentional rather than silently dropped, and note jpegRepresentation takes the option keyed as CIImageRepresentationOption (real code), not the raw String the architecture sketch uses.
- deriveRecipe's Task.detached captures self strongly and the spec leaves it unaddressed. Real code (AppViewModel.swift:343) `Task.detached { ... self.derivedLUT / self.sourceImage / self.selectLUT(lut) ... }` with no [weak self]. The spec only changes the success-branch guard to `if self.source != nil` and says RecipeExtractor is untouched, but does not reconcile this strong-self capture with the `[weak self, engine]` discipline it applies everywhere else - a (minor) retain for the duration of a long derive, and an inconsistency the refactor should fix while it is rewriting that branch anyway.
- WorkingSpace is oversold as 'the single working-space seam.' It governs LUT interpolation space (CubeLUT.makeFilter inputColorSpace) and output encoding space (createCGImage/tiff/png/jpeg), but NOT source decode: standard images go through CIImage(contentsOf:) (kept, ImageProcessor.swift:59) which decodes in the file's embedded profile and Core Image converts to the CIContext working space, which the spec deliberately leaves at the CI default. So a P3/AdobeRGB-tagged JPEG is still funneled to sRGB for LUT interp regardless of WorkingSpace - same as today, not a regression, but the 'single seam, future-proof for P3' framing implies source interpretation is covered when it is not.
- CIRAWFilter is constructed 3x per RAW open. prepareSource->probeRAW builds one CIRAWFilter for capabilities; openImage then renders the neutral 'original' (2nd CIRAWFilter inside buildImage); then scheduleRender renders the live document (3rd CIRAWFilter). CIRAWFilter init does full decoder+metadata setup, so this is a real per-open cost the spec never addresses, on top of the extra full decode for the cached original. Functionally correct but wasteful given the spec's emphasis on responsiveness.

**Open questions:**
- Package refactor blast radius: adding tests REQUIRES splitting the single executableTarget into LUTzyKit (library) + a thin LUTzy executable holding only @main, and making ContentView/AppViewModel public. Confirm the team accepts this restructure (it touches Package.swift, moves LUTzyApp.swift, and adds a few `public`s) — it is the standard SPM way and there is no lighter alternative for @testable.
- New-image document policy: keep the current EditDocument across opens (so a power user A/Bs the same look across a folder) or reset to default on each open? Spec defaults to KEEP. Filmstrip browsing makes this user-visible; needs product sign-off.
- Adjustment list semantics: allow duplicate cases (two Exposure nodes) via append, or enforce one-of-each fixed slots? Spec allows duplicates (more powerful, simpler). Confirm desired.
- Numeric TextField behavior: `.number` parsing is locale-aware and can exceed slider bounds; decide clamp-on-commit and per-knob precision (Kelvin integer vs EV 2dp). Spec shows 2-dp default; refine per row.
- ExportFormat ownership: it currently lives as `ImageProcessor.ExportFormat`. If the render component dissolves ImageProcessor into RenderEngine, ExportFormat must move (e.g. top-level enum) and ContentView's `ForEach(ImageProcessor.ExportFormat.allCases)` + the VM's `exportFormat` type update accordingly. Confirm the new home.
- data: import path: Photos imports have no URL. Spec routes them through a temp file so ImageSource stays URL-only/Sendable. Confirm acceptable vs adding an ImageSource.data(Data) kind.
- RAW fixture in CI: derive-invariance and RAW-parity tests need a license-clean small .dng + .jpg committed under Tests/.../Fixtures. If none can be committed, those tests are skip-by-default and the guarantee is only enforced locally.
- Inspector toggle shortcut: ⌘⇧I and ⌘⌥I are already taken (import from Photos / import folder). Spec proposes ⌘⌃I for the inspector; confirm no other conflict.
- macOS floor: `.inspector` is macOS 14+ — fine given Package.swift platforms `.macOS(.v14)`. If a 13 floor is ever needed this must become a third NavigationSplitView column or an HStack pane.

**Risks:**
- BIGGEST RISK — test target needs a Package restructure: SPM cannot @testable-import an executable's @main module. The library split (LUTzyKit) is mandatory and ripples (move LUTzyApp.swift, add `public` to ContentView/AppViewModel/Notification.Name). If done wrong the app stops building. Mitigation: do the split as its own commit, verify `swift build` runs the app before adding any tests.
- Brief inaccuracies that could waste effort: (a) PreviewView has NO undefined `preview`/FIXME — it compiles; do not invent a fix. (b) RAW neutral decode is already a single shared static (`ImageProcessor.developRAWNeutral`), and extensions are already unified in `ImageProcessor.supportedExtensions`; the 'two sources of truth' cleanup is already done. Building against the brief instead of the real files would add churn.
- KeyMonitor focus leak: if `isEditingTextField` is not reliably reset on blur (FocusState race when clicking field->canvas), single-key shortcuts stay suppressed or fire while typing. Mitigation: the first-responder `NSText` fallback in `handle`, plus setting the flag on every control's `.onChange(of: focused)`.
- Dependency on render/state component signatures: the inspector + tests assume EXACT shapes — `RenderEngine.renderPreview(source:document:maxSize:space:) -> CGImage?`, `export(source:document:to:format:space:quality:)`, `prepareSource(url:) -> (ImageSource, RAWCapabilities)?`, `register(_:for:)`, `AdjustmentNode.apply(to:)`, `CubeLUT.makeFilter(colorSpace:)`. If those land differently, both the wiring and the tests need matching edits. prepareSource/probeRAW are additions THIS component requires from RenderEngine.
- GPU non-determinism in parity/identity tests: Core Image is not bit-exact across drivers. Mitigation: tests use a software-renderer CIContext and small abs tolerances (tol 2; looser for resampled comparisons). Residual flake risk remains on unusual configs.
- Combine debounce fires objectWillChange on every keystroke/drag; `.removeDuplicates()` on an Equatable document caps redundant renders, but it compares the whole document (incl. the adjustments array) each tick — O(n). n is tiny so fine; flagged if node count grows large.
- Side-by-side now keys off `document.lut.lutID != nil`. If a user applies only RAW develop / tone adjustments with no LUT, side-by-side shows single view (matches today's LUT-gated behavior). Confirm whether side-by-side should trigger on ANY non-neutral document instead.
- `currentLUT` mirror can desync from `document.lut.lutID` if any code sets the document's lutID without going through selectLUT (e.g. the Picker's direct `set`). Mitigation: route the Picker through selectLUT, or derive currentLUT lazily by resolving lutID against library.allLUTs + the lutIDs map rather than caching.

## 4. Migration & sequencing

## Ordered Migration Plan — each step builds, passes, and is independently shippable

Guiding principle: **introduce the new spine UNDER the old behavior first, then cut over leaf-by-leaf, then delete the old path last.** The old single-shot `applyLUT` keeps working until Step 5/6. Steps 0–4 add code without changing runtime behavior; 5–9 cut over; 10–12 are additive polish.

---

### Step 0 — Test harness + package split (enabling, no behavior change)
- In `Package.swift`: split into a `LUTzyKit` library target (path `LUTzy/`, minus `LUTzyApp.swift`) + a thin `LUTzy` executable holding only `@main`. Add `.testTarget(name:"LUTzyTests", dependencies:["LUTzyKit"], path:"Tests/LUTzyTests")`. Add `public` to the types tests import.
- Add ONE smoke test (`CubeLUT` parse→write round-trip on a 2×2×2 inline `.cube`) to prove the harness runs.
- **Ship gate:** `swift build` runs the app unchanged; `swift test` green. Own commit so a broken split is isolated. No lighter alternative exists (SPM can't `@testable`-import an executable).

### Step 1 — Color seam (`WorkingSpace`), no behavior change
- Add `LUTzy/Models/WorkingSpace.swift`: `enum WorkingSpace { case sRGB, displayP3; var cgColorSpace; static let current = .sRGB }`.
- Refactor the 3 sRGB literals to read through it WITHOUT changing values: `CubeLUT.makeFilter()`→`makeFilter(colorSpace:)` (keep `-> CIFilter?`; callers pass `WorkingSpace.current.cgColorSpace`); `ImageProcessor.export:180` uses it; **`ImageProcessor.renderPreview`'s `createCGImage:109` now passes `WorkingSpace.current.cgColorSpace` explicitly** (closes the implicit-4th-site latent mismatch — verified harmless at sRGB today, correct going forward). Pin `RecipeExtractor`'s sRGB literal (line 81) to `WorkingSpace.sRGB` (NOT `.current`) with a doc comment on the lockstep contract.
- **Ship gate:** identical output at sRGB; parity test asserting preview-raster bytes == export bytes for a known image. Still single-shot.

### Step 2 — Value-state types, defined but UNUSED
- Add `LUTzy/Models/EditDocument.swift` (`EditDocument` incl. a `version:Int=1`, `RAWDevelopSettings`, closed `enum AdjustmentNode`, `LUTSettings`) and `LUTzy/Models/RenderTypes.swift` (`ImageSource` with `Backing{case url; case data}`, `RenderScale`, `LUTID`). Move `ImageError` here as top-level `Sendable` (already top-level in `ImageProcessor:219`, source-compatible).
- **`LUTID` wraps `String`**; add `lutID: LUTID` to `CubeLUT` set inside BOTH explicit inits (file-backed → `LUTID(id)` deterministic; derived → `LUTID(id)`). Add `: Sendable` to `CubeLUT`.
- `RAWDevelopSettings.apply(to:)` uses the VERIFIED set: gate `sharpness/moire/contrast/detail/localToneMap/color+luminance NR` on their real `is*Supported` flags; set `extendedDynamicRangeAmount` UNGUARDED (inherits macOS-12 floor); OMIT `enableEDR`; put highlight recovery behind `if #available(macOS 16,*)` (or omit v1); `gamutMappingEnabled` settable; `.neutral`=all-nil=byte-identical to `developRAWNeutral`.
- Unit-test `AdjustmentNode` ordering, `EditDocument` Codable round-trip, `.neutral` equality. **No app code references these yet.**
- **Ship gate:** compiles, tests green, runtime unchanged.

### Step 3 — Pure pipeline + intensity, defined but UNUSED
- Add `LUTzy/Models/RenderPipeline.swift`: `static func buildImage(source:document:lut:scale:space:) -> CIImage?` (source → RAW develop via a `CIRAWFilter` rebuilt from URL → ordered nodes → LUT-with-intensity). RAW preview uses `CIRAWFilter.scaleFactor` early-downscale; standard uses early Lanczos.
- Add `CubeLUT.makeFilter(colorSpace:)` filter cache (keyed by colorSpace name) and `applyWithIntensity(to:intensity:colorSpace:)` (short-circuit 0/1; else `CIDissolveTransition` `.cropped(to: input.extent)`; handle `makeFilter` returning nil by returning input). **Cache lives on the actor (Step 4), NOT on the `CubeLUT` value** — keep `CubeLUT` an immutable `Sendable` value; the engine owns a `[LUTID×space: CIFilter]` cache. (Resolves the "@unchecked box on a value type" question by avoiding it.)
- Test: identity pipeline (empty doc, no LUT) ≈ source; intensity endpoints; dissolve extent preserved. **Document the intensity blend's true space honestly:** the dissolve mixes in the CIContext working space (CI default ≈ linear-light; runtime-verified red/blue midpoint ≈ 188, not 128), NOT the `colorSpace` arg — that arg governs only cube interpolation. Fix the design comment accordingly.
- **Ship gate:** compiles, tests green, runtime unchanged.

### Step 4 — `actor RenderEngine`, wired BEHIND the old path
- Add `LUTzy/Models/RenderEngine.swift`: actor owning the single Metal `CIContext`; `renderPreview(...) -> sending CGImage?`, `export(...) throws`, `thumbnail(...) async -> sending CGImage?`, `register/unregister(lut:for:)`, and **`makeSource(url:) async -> ImageSource?`** (measures native extent, classifies RAW via the now-widened `ImageProcessor.rawExtensions`). Move `ExportFormat` to a top-level `Sendable, CaseIterable, Identifiable` enum — **PRESERVE `Identifiable`+`id`+"TIFF"/"JPEG"/"PNG" raw values** (`ContentView:79` Picker + `AppViewModel:29` need them) and update BOTH references.
- `ImageProcessor` keeps its GPU methods FOR NOW; the engine is added alongside. Add a `RenderEngining` protocol the VM holds (default `.shared`) so tests inject a fake.
- Test engine in isolation (synthetic gradient through empty doc → non-nil `CGImage`).
- **Ship gate:** both `ImageProcessor.context` and `RenderEngine.context` exist briefly (functional, slightly wasteful). App still runs the OLD path. Safe coexistence point.

### Step 5 — Cut PREVIEW over to the engine (first user-visible change)
- In `AppViewModel`: add `@Published var document: EditDocument`, non-published `var source: ImageSource?`, a `RenderEngining` dependency. Keep **computed** `var sourceImage: CIImage?`/`hasSource` and `var selectedLUT: CubeLUT?` shims so `PreviewView`(14,15,54,72,91,94,106) and `ContentView`(131) keep compiling.
- Rewrite `openImage(url:)` to build `source` via `engine.makeSource` and render preview via `engine.renderPreview` off the main actor, writing `previewNSImage = NSImage(cgImage:)` on the main actor. Replace `applyLUT` with a **debounced `scheduleRender`** gated by `EditDocument` `Equatable`; `selectLUT` mutates `document.lut` + registers by `LUTID`. Debounce ONLY continuous edits; open/navigate render immediately. Staleness guard compares the LIVE `document` and encodes `isShowingOriginal` so A/B toggles aren't swallowed by the early-return.
- **Handle the Photos `openImage(data:name:):116` path NOW** via `ImageSource.Backing.data(Data)` (Data is `Sendable`) — `sourceURL` is nil for these; temp-files misclassify RAW.
- Key the per-image edit store by `ImageCollection.Item.id` (UUID, line 9), NOT URL (URL is nil for Photos items).
- **Ship gate:** preview reflects RAW develop + adjustments + intensity. Export STILL uses old `processedImage` path. Manual smoke + parity test on a standard image.

### Step 6 — Cut EXPORT over to the engine; delete `processedImage`
- Rewrite `exportDialog` to `await engine.export(...)`. Remove the `[processor, exportFormat]` `Task.detached:297` capture and `imageToExport = processedImage ?? sourceImage!:283`. Fix main-actor isolation in the `panel.begin`/completion (route through a `@MainActor func performExport`; reset `isExporting` in the SAME continuation as the status update, not a separate `defer`+`Task`). Pass real JPEG `quality` (closes the wiring gap; default 0.95 preserved).
- Delete the now-unused `processedImage`/`selectedLUT` STORED properties (keep computed shims). Re-grep readers first.
- **Ship gate:** export honors develop+intensity at full res. **Parity test on the SAME `EditDocument`** (standard image, full vs preview scale, tolerance). RAW parity is tolerance-based by design (draft demosaic) — add a RAW *symmetry* test (a develop knob changes BOTH paths), not byte-equality.

### Step 7 — Move thumbnails + finish dissolving `ImageProcessor` GPU duties
- Migrate BOTH `ImageCollection` thumbnail sites — the `Task.detached` in `generateThumbnails()` AND the synchronous `addFromData` — to `await RenderEngine.shared.thumbnail(...)`; make `addFromData` async/Task-wrapped. Wrap returned `CGImage` as `NSImage` on the main actor.
- Strip `context`/`renderPreview`/`renderToNSImage`/`export`/`generateThumbnail` from `ImageProcessor`. Keep the static format vocabulary (`rawExtensions` now non-private, `supportedExtensions`, `supportedTypes`) and `developRAWNeutral`. Make the residue `Sendable`.
- **Ship gate:** exactly ONE `CIContext` in the app. No non-`Sendable` singleton with GPU state remains.

### Step 8 — Flip strict concurrency ON
- Add `swiftSettings:[.enableUpcomingFeature("StrictConcurrency")]` (or `swiftLanguageMode(.v6)` if clean) to BOTH targets. Make `RecipeExtractor.Result`(line 65), `RecipeReport`, `RecipeReport.CameraInfo` `Sendable` (trivial — all-`Sendable` members; the `(dx:Int,dy:Int)` tuple is `Sendable`); make derive's progress callback `@Sendable`. Fix residual captures (e.g. `deriveRecipe`'s strong-`self` `Task.detached:343` → `[weak self]` + main-actor hops).
- **Ship gate:** `swift build`/`swift test` warning-clean under strict concurrency. The Swift-6-readiness milestone — a non-event precisely because Steps 4–7 built the value-state + actor spine.

### Step 9 — Wire derive into the new state (the one new derive task)
- `deriveRecipe` now `engine.register(lut, for: newLUTID)` and sets `document.lut.lutID = newLUTID`. **PRESERVE the surrounding bookkeeping** the concurrency spec dropped: still `CubeLUT.write(...)` the scratch file and set `derivedScratchURL` so `saveDerivedLUT()` keeps working. Add the debug assertion naming `RAWDevelopSettings.neutral` as the baseline contract; `RecipeExtractor` is otherwise UNCHANGED (own `makeContext():421`, own sRGB, no `EditDocument` import).
- **Ship gate:** derive still produces a working LUT, now visible through the document; derive-baseline invariance test (derived LUT identical regardless of `document.rawDevelop`).

### Step 10 — UI: RAW develop + adjustments inspector
- Add `LUTzy/Views/InspectorView.swift` (RAW Develop / Tone & Color / LUT groups) via `.inspector` (macOS 14+). RAW group shown only for `source.kind == .raw`; each row gated by a `RAWCapabilities` value captured at open using the VERIFIED `is*Supported` flags (NOT `isDustRemovalSupported`). LUT group binds `document.lut.intensity` (0–100%) via `Slider(onEditingChanged:)`.
- **Fix the `ContentView` NSEvent KeyMonitor** (`handle:228`) to bail when a text field is first responder (new `isEditingTextField` gate + `NSText` first-responder fallback), so typing `v`/space/`[`/`]` in fields doesn't fire shortcuts. Pick an inspector-toggle shortcut not colliding with `⌘⇧I`/`⌘⌥I` (Photos/folder import).
- **Ship gate:** inspector drives live re-render; shortcuts don't leak into fields.

### Step 11 — Lightweight undo + per-image edits
- Add a **per-image** undo stack (keyed by `Item.id`, NOT global — a global stack corrupts other images' edits on navigate-then-undo) and a testable `EditDocumentStore`.
- **Ship gate:** ⌘Z/⌘⇧Z scoped per image; store unit-tested.

### Step 12 (optional / deferred) — descriptors & metadata
- Promote export to an `ExportRequest` descriptor (format+depth+quality+policy) IF a power-user panel is wanted. Metadata/ICC, if pursued, requires `CGImageDestination` (the `CIImageRepresentationOption` path cannot carry EXIF) and solving orientation double-rotation: standard images load via bare `CIImage(contentsOf:)`/`CIImage(data:)` with NO orientation baking, so bake at load (`kCIImageApplyOrientationProperty`) then force output `orientation=1`; RAW via `CIRAWFilter` already bakes. Genuinely separable; must not block 0–11.

## 5. Cross-cutting concerns

## Cross-Cutting Concerns

### Color management (one seam, threaded not disciplined)
- **Single source of truth:** `WorkingSpace.current` (default `.sRGB`) feeds BOTH the LUT-interpolation space (`CubeLUT.makeFilter:135` inputColorSpace) AND the output/raster encoding space (`createCGImage` + the three export encoders at `ImageProcessor.export:185/195/205`). The SAME value threaded through `buildImage(...space:)` keeps the two in **lockstep** — a P3 flip is one line and cannot desync them. The only residual risk is a future inline-literal refactor; enforce a code-review rule that the only `CGColorSpace.sRGB` literal lives in `WorkingSpace.cgColorSpace`.
- **The implicit 4th site is real and fixed in Step 1:** `renderPreview`'s `createCGImage:109` passes NO color space (CIContext default) while export forces sRGB:180. Verified byte-identical at sRGB *today*, but a latent divergence the moment wide-gamut sources or P3 output appear. Always pass `space.cgColorSpace` to `createCGImage`.
- **Honest scope:** `WorkingSpace` governs LUT-interp and OUTPUT — NOT source decode. Standard images decode via `CIImage(contentsOf:)` in their embedded profile and convert to the CIContext working space (left at CI default). A P3/AdobeRGB JPEG is still funneled to sRGB for LUT-interp, same as today; not a regression, but do not oversell the seam as covering source interpretation.
- **`CIContext` working space deliberately left at CI default.** We do NOT set `kCIContextWorkingColorSpace=extendedLinearSRGB`/`kCIContextWorkingFormat=RGBAh` in v1: it changes intermediate precision/primaries for EVERY render and needs a soft-clip before the 8-bit JPEG/PNG encoders (the 16-bit TIFF path hides out-of-gamut hue shifts). Documented seam for later.
- **Derive stays sRGB, pinned NOT to `.current`.** A derived `.cube` is FIT in the baseline-render space (`RecipeExtractor:81`, sRGB) and APPLIED by `makeFilter` in `WorkingSpace.current`; for self-consistency fit-space must equal apply-space. Both are sRGB today. When P3 ships, derive must re-fit in P3 OR stamp its build space onto the `CubeLUT` so `makeFilter` matches — a tracked follow-up. Never blindly thread `WorkingSpace.current` into the derive sampler (its neutral RAW baseline is itself an sRGB-default render).

### Concurrency / Swift 6 (the GPU is the only isolation boundary)
- **One actor owns the one `CIContext`.** `CIImage`/`CIFilter`/`CIContext` are born and die inside `RenderEngine`; only `Sendable` values cross in (`EditDocument`, `ImageSource`, `WorkingSpace`, `RenderScale`, `LUTID`, `URL`/`Data`) and a `sending CGImage?` or `Data`/`Void` crosses out. Dissolves the three confirmed hazards: the `[processor]` `Task.detached:297` capture in `exportDialog`, the strong-`self` `Task.detached:343` in `deriveRecipe`, and the `@MainActor`-inheriting `Task {}:236` in `applyLUT` that runs the full filter graph on the main thread.
- **`CGImage` is NOT `Sendable`** (verified, Swift 6.3) — the pinned "return `CGImage`, zero `@unchecked`" is contradictory as written. Use **`-> sending CGImage?`** (region-based isolation; available in this toolchain). Preserves BOTH the zero-`@unchecked` promise AND the zero-extra-copy benefit over returning bytes. Confirm at the Step 8 flip; fallback is a single audited `@unchecked Sendable` wrapper.
- **`NSImage` never crosses out of the main actor** — constructed from the returned `CGImage` ON the main actor and stored in `@Published` previews. Guard against regressions (no view passes `previewNSImage` into a Task/actor; none does today).
- **`RecipeExtractor` stays a separate detached job** with its OWN `CIContext` (`makeContext():421`; it never touched the singleton). Make `RecipeExtractor.Result` + `RecipeReport` + `RecipeReport.CameraInfo` `Sendable` (trivial) and its progress callback `@Sendable` so `.value` crosses cleanly.
- **Debounce + change-detection** (`EditDocument: Equatable`) coalesces continuous slider drags; apply ONLY to continuous edits, never to discrete open/navigate (no latency penalty stepping the filmstrip). Optionally thread `Task.isCancelled` into the actor between pipeline stages for finer GPU savings.
- **`developRAWNeutral` must stay inside an isolation domain** (actor-side in `buildImage`, own-context in derive). Add a doc comment / debug assertion it is not called on `@MainActor` (heavy decode).

### Preview/export parity (structural, not conventional)
- Parity becomes a property of ONE `buildImage` evaluated by ONE actor through ONE `WorkingSpace`, with `RenderScale` the ONLY difference. The OLD two-path agreement is replaced by one path.
- **Resolution-independence invariant:** every `AdjustmentNode` case MUST use normalized/extent-relative units (the current set — exposure, color controls, highlight/shadow, temp/tint, vibrance — are all scale-invariant). Any FUTURE pixel-sized node (grain/blur/sharpen) MUST express radius in normalized units or early-downscaled preview and full-res export diverge silently. State in the node spec; enforce in review. (Note: `CITemperatureAndTint` as a creative slider feels inverted — raising target Kelvin cools the image relative to a fixed 6500 source; decide the UI mapping before shipping that node.)
- **RAW parity is tolerance-based by design:** `CIRAWFilter.scaleFactor`/draft demosaic differs subtly from full decode, so a scaled RAW preview is not byte-proof of export. v1 accepts the small interaction delta; enhancement is two-phase (draft while dragging, settle full-quality on idle). Tests assert *wiring symmetry* (a knob affects both paths) rather than byte-equality for RAW.

### Zero-dependency (Apple frameworks only)
- Everything used is first-party: SwiftUI, CoreImage (`CIRAWFilter`, `CIColorCubeWithColorSpace`, `CIDissolveTransition`, typed `CIFilter` builtins — `RecipeExtractor` already imports `CoreImage.CIFilterBuiltins`), AppKit (`NSImage`/`NSEvent`), ImageIO/CoreGraphics (`CGImageSource`, `CGColorSpace`, and `CGImageDestination` IF metadata is later pursued), Metal (`MTLCreateSystemDefaultDevice`), simd (derive), Charts (`RecipeReportView`). No third-party packages; the `Package.swift` `dependencies` array stays empty. Tests use XCTest. The library/executable split adds no dependency, only target structure.

## 6. Risk register

| Risk | Likelihood | Mitigation |
|---|---|---|
| **`CGImage` not `Sendable`** breaks the actor's pinned return type under Swift 6 — the central concurrency promise | High | Use `-> sending CGImage?` (verified available, Swift 6.3). Confirm at the Step 8 strict-concurrency flip. Fallback: one audited `@unchecked Sendable` wrapper, centralized. |
| **`LUTID` minted as random UUID** → file-backed LUTs get a new ID on every `LUTLibrary.scan` (rebuilds all `CubeLUT`; triggered by `saveDerivedLUT`), so persisted/undo `document.lut.lutID` stops resolving (silent LUT loss) | High | `LUTID` wraps `String`, **deterministic from `CubeLUT.id`/path** for file-backed LUTs; only in-memory derived LUTs get a synthetic id. Test resolution survives a simulated rescan. |
| **RAW-develop spec's fabricated/inverted SDK names** copied verbatim (`enableEDR`/`isEDRModeEnabled`/`isDustRemovalSupported`/`isBaselineExposureAvailable`; one spec wrongly says sharpness/moire "don't exist") → compile errors or missing gates | High | Use the header-verified set. Single EDR knob `extendedDynamicRangeAmount` UNGUARDED (inherits macOS-12 floor); highlight-recovery behind `#available(macOS 16)`; all real `is*Supported` used; `gamutMappingEnabled` settable. Compile Step 2 early. |
| **Orientation double-rotation** if metadata writing is added and source EXIF orientation is copied onto already-upright pixels (standard load is bare `CIImage(contentsOf:)`/`CIImage(data:)`, NO baking) | High (only if metadata pursued) | Defer to Step 12. When done: bake at load via `kCIImageApplyOrientationProperty`, then force output `orientation=1`; test RAW + standard. RAW already bakes. |
| **Global undo stack** corrupts other images' edits (navigate → ⌘Z writes prior image's doc onto current `Item.id`) | High | Per-image undo keyed by `Item.id` (Step 11). Unit-test navigate-then-undo isolation. |
| **Photos/`data:` import** (`openImage(data:):116`, `sourceURL=nil`) breaks engine render/export + per-image edits if `ImageSource` is URL-only | High | `ImageSource.Backing{case url; case data}` (Data is `Sendable`); store keyed by `Item.id`. Handle in Step 5 — shipped code path. |
| **Package-restructure (library split) done wrong** stops the app building | Medium | Step 0 as its own commit; verify `swift build` runs the app BEFORE adding tests. Standard SPM pattern; no alternative for `@testable`. |
| **`ExportFormat` move loses `Identifiable`/raw values** → `ContentView:79` Picker fails; missed `AppViewModel:29` reference | Medium | Keep `Identifiable+id+"TIFF"/"JPEG"/"PNG"` when promoting to top-level; update BOTH references in the same commit (Step 4). Re-grep before deleting from `ImageProcessor`. |
| **EXIF/ICC via `CIImageRepresentationOption` silently ignored** (`CIContext.h` enum has no `kCGImageProperty*` channel) | Medium (only if metadata pursued) | Verified against the header. Use `CGImageDestination` for metadata. Scope as Step 12. |
| **LUT-interp vs output space desync** from a future inline-literal refactor | Medium | Thread ONE `WorkingSpace` through `buildImage`; both sites read `request.space`. Review rule: no inline `CGColorSpace.sRGB` except `WorkingSpace.cgColorSpace`. |
| **Derive baseline drift** — a future param lets `document.rawDevelop` reach `RecipeExtractor` | Medium | Physical decoupling (no `EditDocument` import) + `deriveRecipe`/`derive` signatures take only URLs/options + `.neutral` debug assertion + a grep/compile test that fails if the derive signature gains a develop param. |
| **Stale `processedImage`/A-B caching** shows a pre-edit graded image during Space-hold after an edit | Medium | Encode `isShowingOriginal` into the render-request identity; invalidate any graded cache on every render write-back, or render an `intensity=0` document for "original". |
| **Debounce applied to navigation/open** adds latency to filmstrip stepping; `activate()` double-schedules | Medium | Debounce ONLY continuous edits; open/navigate use an immediate (non-debounced) path; don't both set-document-silently AND call scheduleRender. |
| **`KeyMonitor` steals keystrokes** from new inspector text fields (typing `v`/space fires shortcuts) | Medium | `isEditingTextField` gate + `NSText` first-responder fallback in `KeyMonitor.handle:228` (Step 10); reset flag on every control's focus change. |
| **`makeFilter` returns `CIFilter?`** but pipeline may consume non-optional | Medium | Keep `-> CIFilter?` (the `CIFilter(name:)` guard at `CubeLUT:132` is correct defensive code) and handle nil in `applyWithIntensity` (return input). Don't force-unwrap. |
| **Silent LUT-application failure** — deleting the old `"LUT application failed"` branch (`AppViewModel:238`) with a non-optional `applyWithIntensity` hides a broken `.cube` | Medium | One-time validity check at parse/load (report once), not per-render; confirm silent-fallback is acceptable for arbitrary user `.cube` files. |
| **`CIRAWFilter` constructed 3× per RAW open** (probe + neutral original + live doc) — wasteful given responsiveness goals | Low | Acceptable v1; cache `RAWCapabilities` at open in `@Published`; do not re-probe per render. Revisit only if profiling shows init dominates. |
| **GPU non-determinism** flakes parity/identity tests across drivers | Low | Software-renderer `CIContext` in tests + small abs tolerances (looser for resampled comparisons). |
| **`addFromData` synchronous thumbnail** missed when migrating to actor (second, easily-overlooked site) | Low | Step 7 explicitly migrates BOTH `ImageCollection` sites; `FilmstripView` has none. |
| **Two `CIContext`s coexist** during Steps 4–6 transition | Low | Intentional, functional; eliminated in Step 7. Keep it short. |

## 7. Open questions

## Consolidated Open Questions (deduped, with recommendations)

**Resolved by ground-truth verification — recorded so they are not re-litigated:**
- *Which CIRAWFilter properties/gates exist + their availability?* → Header-verified (`MacOSX26.5.sdk`): class is `NS_CLASS_AVAILABLE(12_0,15_0)`; full `is*Supported` set EXISTS; `gamutMappingEnabled` settable; EDR is the single `extendedDynamicRangeAmount` (0...2) with NO availability macro → callable UNGUARDED on the v14 target; highlight-recovery is `NS_AVAILABLE(16_0)` (needs `#available`); `isDustRemovalSupported`/`isBaselineExposureAvailable`/`enableEDR` do NOT exist.
- *Xcode project vs SPM?* → SPM only. Strict concurrency = `swiftSettings`; tests = `.testTarget` + library split.
- *Two extension lists / `isImageFile` / neutral-baseline duplication / PreviewView FIXME?* → All non-existent / already unified. Do not chase.
- *Is the intensity blend in the working space?* → No (runtime-verified ≈ linear-light midpoint). Document truthfully; the `colorSpace` arg governs only cube interpolation.
- *`CubeLUT.id` type for `LUTID`?* → `String`. `LUTID` wraps `String`, deterministic for file-backed LUTs.

**Genuine product/scope decisions (need sign-off):**
1. **`kCIContextWorkingColorSpace` precision** — set `extendedLinearSRGB`+`RGBAh`? Requires a soft-clip before 8-bit JPEG/PNG encoders or out-of-gamut hue shifts/banding (TIFF16 hides them). **Recommend: defer; CI default for v1.**
2. **Display P3 + derive build-space stamp** — flipping `WorkingSpace.current` to `.displayP3` moves LUT-interp/output in lockstep, BUT derived LUTs are fit in sRGB and would mis-map. **Recommend: keep sRGB v1; the concrete prerequisite is a `buildSpace` field on `CubeLUT` consulted by `makeFilter` (or re-fit derive in P3).**
3. **Intensity blend space sign-off** — the dissolve mixes in the CIContext working space (≈ linear), NEW behavior with no prior. **Recommend: quick visual check at ~50% on a few LUTs; if changed later it is a versioned look change for every sub-100% render.**
4. **Metadata/ICC + orientation on export** — currently zero metadata is written. Reliable preservation needs `CGImageDestination` + orientation baking, incl. a GPS-privacy sub-decision (`.preserveExceptGPS`?). **Recommend: defer to Step 12.**
5. **Export descriptor scope** — ship an `ExportRequest` (format+depth+quality+policy) + panel now, or headless defaults (preserve-off, 0.95, per-container depth)? **Recommend: thread `quality` now (cheap); full descriptor/panel later.**
6. **Edit persistence across launches** — `EditDocument` is `Codable` to enable sidecar/app-state persistence; v1 in-memory only. **Recommend: defer; if pursued, depends on the stable `LUTID` (already designed) and stable file-backed LUT ids.**
7. **New-image document policy** — KEEP the current `EditDocument` across opens (A/B a look across a folder) or RESET per open? **Recommend: KEEP; product to confirm (filmstrip-visible).**
8. **Undo granularity** — per-image (recommended) vs single global stack (data-loss risk). **Recommend: per-image, keyed by `Item.id`.**
9. **Adjustment list semantics** — allow duplicate node cases (two Exposure nodes) via append, or one-of-each fixed slots? **Recommend: allow duplicates (more powerful, simpler).**
10. **"Original" definition for A/B + side-by-side** — develop-applied-but-no-adjustments-no-LUT vs neutral-RAW-defaults? And should side-by-side trigger on ANY non-neutral document or only when a LUT is set (today's `selectedLUT != nil` gate)? **Recommend: original = develop-applied, no adjustments/LUT; keep LUT-gated side-by-side for v1.**
11. **Photos `data:` handling** — `ImageSource.Backing.data(Data)` (recommended) vs temp-file. **Recommend: the enum — temp files misclassify RAW and need cleanup.**
12. **Debounce interval** — start ~16–80 ms; tune on the largest expected RAW (45MP+). Consider draft-then-settle. **Recommend: tune empirically; discrete nav bypasses it.**
13. **RAW fixtures in CI** — derive-invariance + RAW-parity tests need a license-clean small `.dng`+`.jpg` under `Tests/.../Fixtures`; else `XCTSkip` (guarantee local-only). **Recommend: commit a tiny pair if licensing allows.**
14. **Inspector toggle shortcut** — `⌘⇧I`/`⌘⌥I` are taken (Photos/folder import per `PreviewView:132`). Pick a non-colliding chord; verify against the full `KeyMonitor` map. 
15. **`CITemperatureAndTint` slider mapping** — as a creative WB control it inverts vs typical editors (only `targetNeutral` set, fixed 6500 source). **Recommend: decide direction/labeling before shipping that node; identity at (6500,0) is safe as a seed.**
16. **Filter-cache eviction** — cache confirmed on the actor (keeps `CubeLUT` a clean `Sendable` value); only open point is eviction policy under many LUTs. **Recommend: simple per-`LUTID×space` dictionary; revisit eviction only if memory grows.**
17. **Silent LUT-failure UX** — keep silent fallback-to-original (drops today's `"LUT application failed"` status) or report once at load? **Recommend: validity check at parse/load.**
